196:                                       "matched_text": window,
197:                                       "matched_alias": hits[0]["alias"]})
198:                     matched.update(range(i, i + size))   # words of a longer name are not read again
199:                     continue
200: 
201:                 if window.lower() in self.targets:
202:                     t = self.targets[window.lower()]
203:                     if ("Target", t["target_id"]) not in seen:
204:                         seen.add(("Target", t["target_id"]))
205:                         found.append({"type": "Target", "id": t["target_id"],
206:                                       "label": t["symbol"], "matched_text": window})
207:                     matched.update(range(i, i + size))   # words of a longer name are not read again
208:                     continue

211:                     cid = self.companies[key][0]
212:                     if ("Company", cid) not in seen:

232:         """Token spans that name exactly one drug, longest first, not overlapping."""

+"""Round-5 independent HTTP confirmations and bounded latency/lock measurements."""
+import concurrent.futures
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import socket
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import threading
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+PORT = 8880
+BASE = f'http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}'
+DB = ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite'
+
+def post(path, text):
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    request = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=json.dumps({'text': text}).encode(),
+                                     headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
+    try:
+        response = urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=30)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
+        response = error
+    raw = response.read()
+    return {'path': path, 'text': text, 'characters': len(text), 'status': response.status,
+            'seconds': time.perf_counter() - start, 'bytes': len(raw), 'body': json.loads(raw)}
+
+with socket.socket() as sock:
+    assert sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', PORT)) != 0, 'audit port occupied'
+before = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1',
+       'PYTHONIOENCODING': 'utf-8', 'TEMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'), 'TMP': str(OUT / 'tmp')}
+with (OUT / 'm5_server.txt').open('w', encoding='utf-8') as log:
+    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-B', '-u', 'src/app.py', str(PORT)],
+                            cwd=ROOT, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+                            creationflags=getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0))
+    try:
+        for _ in range(100):
+            assert proc.poll() is None
+            try:
+                urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
+                break
+            except OSError:
+                time.sleep(.1)
+        else:
+            raise RuntimeError('server unavailable')
+        inputs = [
+            'bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab - awwb/ osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab-awwb / osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab - awwb / osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab-awwb| osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab-awwb; osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab-awwb and osimertinib',
+            'liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin',
+            'liposomal and nanoparticle formulations of doxorubicin',
+            'liposomal and sustained release doxorubicin',
+            'biosimilar and investigational version of bevacizumab',
+            'Erlotinib, hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'Erlotinib; hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'Erlotinib and hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'Erlotinib hydrochloride', 'erlotinib',
+            'Pegylated interferon alfa-2a', 'PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A',
+            'CHEMBL1201560', 'EGFR biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'AstraZeneca biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'osimertinib\nbiosimilar bevacizumab',
+        ]
+        cases = [post(path, value) for value in inputs
+                 for path in ['/api/explore', '/api/resolve']]
+        for value in [
+            'Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?',
+            'Which companies sponsor bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?',
+            'Which trials evaluate liposomal and nanoparticle formulations of doxorubicin?',
+            'Which trials evaluate liposomal and sustained release doxorubicin?',
+            'Which trials evaluate biosimilar and investigational version of bevacizumab?',
+            'Which trials evaluate Erlotinib, hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab?',
+            'Which trials evaluate Pegylated interferon alfa-2a?',
+            'Which trials evaluate PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A?',
+            'Which trials evaluate osimertinib and biosimilar bevacizumab?',
+            'Which companies sponsor osimertinib and biosimilar bevacizumab?',
+        ]:
+            cases.append(post('/api/ask', value))
+        (OUT / 'm5_http.json').write_text(json.dumps({'base': BASE, 'cases': cases},
+            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+        for case in cases:
+            body = case['body']
+            if case['path'] != '/api/resolve':
+                print(json.dumps({'text': case['text'], 'path': case['path'],
+                    'center': body.get('center', {}).get('id'), 'refused': body.get('refused'),
+                    'also': body.get('also'), 'evidence_count': len(body.get('evidence', [])),
+                    'first_edge': (body.get('evidence') or [{}])[0].get('edge_id')},
+                    ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)
+        slow = ' '.join(['x'] * 250)
+        sequential = []
+        for path, text in [('/api/explore', 'EGFR'), ('/api/explore', slow),
+                           ('/api/ask', slow), ('/api/explore', ' '.join(['x']*244+['osimertinib'])),
+                           ('/api/explore', ' '.join(['x']*251))]:
+            sequential.append(post(path, text))
+        # Four simultaneous allowed 499-character requests contend for the actual engine lock.
+        barrier = threading.Barrier(4)
+        def send_slow(_):
+            barrier.wait()
+            return post('/api/explore', slow)
+        started = time.perf_counter()
+        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
+            concurrent_rows = list(pool.map(send_slow, range(4)))
+        elapsed = time.perf_counter()-started
+        # Readable normal request behind one long lookup; .1 s is a measured scheduling gap,
+        # not a claim that a specific internal lock acquisition has been observed.
+        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
+            future = pool.submit(post, '/api/explore', slow)
+            time.sleep(.1)
+            follower = post('/api/explore', 'EGFR')
+            leader = future.result()
+        result = {'sequential': sequential, 'concurrent': concurrent_rows,
+                  'concurrent_wall_seconds': elapsed, 'normal_behind_slow': {'leader': leader, 'follower': follower},
+                  'normal_after': post('/api/explore', 'EGFR')}
+        (OUT / 'perf_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+        print(json.dumps({'performance_seconds': [r['seconds'] for r in sequential],
+                          'concurrent_seconds': [r['seconds'] for r in concurrent_rows],
+                          'concurrent_wall_seconds': elapsed, 'normal_behind_slow_seconds': follower['seconds']}))
+    finally:
+        proc.terminate()
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+        with socket.socket() as sock:
+            closed = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', PORT)) != 0
+        after = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+        (OUT / 'm5_http_lifecycle.json').write_text(json.dumps({'pid': proc.pid, 'port': PORT,
+            'server_exit': proc.returncode, 'port_closed': closed, 'database_unchanged': before == after,
+            'db_sha256_before': before, 'db_sha256_after': after}, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+        assert closed and before == after

+"""Bounded subprocess benchmarks; writes confined to m5 and never calls a model."""
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+import types
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
+
+if len(sys.argv) > 1:
+    import query
+    import textrules as T
+    mode, kind, size = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3])
+    module = query
+    if mode == 'm4':
+        module = types.ModuleType('perf_m4_query')
+        module.__file__ = str(ROOT / 'src/query.py')
+        sys.modules[module.__name__] = module
+        code = subprocess.run(['git', 'show', 'bf436c6:src/query.py'], cwd=ROOT,
+                              capture_output=True, check=True).stdout.decode('utf-8')
+        exec(compile(code, module.__file__, 'exec'), module.__dict__)
+    kg = module.OncoKG()
+    kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
+    text = {'plain': ' '.join(['x'] * size),
+            'drug': ' '.join(['x'] * size + ['osimertinib']),
+            'split': ';'.join(['x'] * size),
+            'single': 'x' * size,
+            'late_qualifier': ' '.join(['x'] * size + ['biosimilar', 'bevacizumab'])}[kind]
+    calls = {'is_distinct_product': 0, 'is_radiotracer': 0, 'characters': 0}
+    for name in ['is_distinct_product', 'is_radiotracer']:
+        original = getattr(T, name)
+        def wrapped(value, name=name, original=original):
+            calls[name] += 1
+            calls['characters'] += len(value)
+            return original(value)
+        setattr(T, name, wrapped)
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    entities = kg.link_entities(text)
+    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
+    kg.conn.close()
+    print(json.dumps({'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'characters': len(text),
+                     'seconds': elapsed, 'calls': calls, 'entities': entities}))
+else:
+    env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1',
+           'PYTHONIOENCODING': 'utf-8', 'TEMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'),
+           'TMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'), 'TMPDIR': str(OUT / 'tmp')}
+    cases = [('plain', n) for n in [50, 100, 200, 250, 500, 1000]]
+    cases += [('drug', 244), ('split', 250), ('single', 500), ('late_qualifier', 235)]
+    rows = []
+    for kind, size in cases:
+        for mode in ['m4', 'HEAD']:
+            try:
+                proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-B', __file__, mode, kind, str(size)],
+                                      cwd=ROOT, env=env, capture_output=True, timeout=20)
+                row = json.loads(proc.stdout) if proc.returncode == 0 else {
+                    'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'error': proc.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace')}
+            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+                row = {'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'timeout_seconds': 20}
+            rows.append(row)
+            (OUT / 'perf_results.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+            print(json.dumps(row), flush=True)

149:     r"|\bgeneric\s+version\b"
150:     r"|\b(?:injection|solution|product)\s+of\s+[a-z]+mab\b",
151:     re.I,
152: )
153: 
154: # "Bevacizumab-Pfizer", "Trastuzumab-dkst": a hyphenated sponsor name or an
155: # FDA four-letter suffix on an INN stem.
156: RE_BIOSIMILAR_SUFFIX = re.compile(
157:     r"^[A-Za-z]{6,}(?:mab|cept|tinib|ase|grastim|stim|poetin)\s*[\-–]\s*(?P<suffix>[A-Za-z]{2,12})$", re.I)
158: 
159: # Words that follow a drug name after a hyphen but are not suffixes at all.
160: # "pembrolizumab-free" is an absence, not a product.
161: SUFFIX_LOOKALIKES = {"free", "only", "like", "base", "type", "form", "dose",
162:                      "naive", "based", "alone", "mono", "plus", "arm"}
163: 
164: # Since 2017 the FDA gives a four-letter suffix to *originator* biologics as
165: # well (cemiplimab-rwlc, dostarlimab-gxly, tislelizumab-jsgr), so a suffix on
166: # its own does not mark a biosimilar. Treating every suffix as one blocked the
167: # originators. A four-letter lowercase suffix is a biosimilar only when it is a
168: # known biosimilar suffix; a longer or capitalised suffix is a sponsor name.
169: BIOSIMILAR_FDA_SUFFIXES = {
170:     # bevacizumab
171:     "awwb", "bvzr", "maly", "adcd", "tnjn", "nwgd",
172:     # trastuzumab
173:     "dkst", "pkrb", "dttb", "qyyp", "anns", "strf",
174:     # rituximab
175:     "abbs", "pvvr", "arrx",
176:     # pegfilgrastim / filgrastim
177:     "jmdb", "cbqv", "bmez", "apgf", "fpgk", "pbbk", "sndz", "aafi", "ayow",
178:     # denosumab
179:     "bbdz", "bmwo", "bnht", "dssb", "kyqq", "nxxp",
180: }
181: 
182: # Biosimilar brand names. A brand is not caught by any pattern; these were
183: # found resolving to their originators (Mvasi, Alymsys, Zirabev -> bevacizumab).
184: BIOSIMILAR_BRANDS = {
185:     "mvasi", "zirabev", "alymsys", "vegzelma", "avzivi", "jobevne", "aybintio",
186:     "onbevzi", "oyavas", "abevmy", "byooviz", "kanjinti", "ogivri", "herzuma",
187:     "ontruzant", "trazimera", "hercessi", "zercepac", "truxima", "ruxience",
188:     "riabni", "rixathon", "udenyca", "fulphila", "ziextenzo", "nyvepria",
189:     "fylnetra", "stimufend", "zarxio", "nivestym", "releuko", "granix",
190: }
191: 
192: 
193: # Reformulated products sold under their own name or code. Checked on the
194: # words of the string, so a brand in brackets ("Paclitaxel (Abraxane)") is seen.
195: REFORMULATION_NAMES = {
196:     "abraxane", "abraxis", "doxil", "caelyx", "lipodox", "onivyde", "depocyt",
197:     "nanopac", "liporaxel", "lipusu", "genexol", "onureg", "cc486",
198:     "pazenir", "abi007", "apealea", "dn101", "asentar",
199: }
200: 
201: 
202: def is_distinct_product(text: str) -> bool:
203:     """True when the string names a product that is not the originator."""
204:     t = (text or "").strip()
205:     if not t:
206:         return False
207:     if RE_DISTINCT_PRODUCT.search(t):
208:         return True
209:     words = set(re.findall(r"[a-z]+", t.lower()))
210:     words |= {canonical_key(w) for w in re.findall(r"[A-Za-z]+[\s\-]?\d+", t)}
211:     if words & (BIOSIMILAR_BRANDS | REFORMULATION_NAMES):
212:         return True
213:     m = RE_BIOSIMILAR_SUFFIX.match(t)
214:     if not m:
215:         return False
216:     suffix = m.group("suffix")
217:     if suffix.lower() in SUFFIX_LOOKALIKES:
218:         return False
219:     # an FDA suffix is four letters, written in either case ("-rwlc", "-RWLC");
220:     # a title-case word is a sponsor name ("-Pfizer", "-Teva")
221:     if len(suffix) == 4 and (suffix.islower() or suffix.isupper()):
222:         return suffix.lower() in BIOSIMILAR_FDA_SUFFIXES

src/app.py
232:             else:
233:                 self._send(404, b"not found", "text/plain")

# Round 5: data, edge, retirement and ordering recheck

Reviewed HEAD: `514929c`. All previously repaired data-layer findings remain **FIXED**. The independent current-graph replay found no adjacency, value, count, grouping or ordering mismatch. Wording/rendering needs the separate page replay; data correctness alone does not establish correct presentation.

## Method and coverage

- Read the m1/m2b/m3/m4 reports and repair record. Reused six m4 `db_*.py` reproducers by copying them unchanged into m5. Each derives its output directory from its own location. Invocations used `python -B`; fixture mutations were only in SQLite memory databases, including backups of the read-only production connection. No model was called.
- [db_recheck.py](db_recheck.py) independently reconstructed adjacency and trial counts from stored rows, then checked **all 6,469 live centres**, **22 role-aware centre/relation/direction/neighbour-type combinations**, exact live edge sets, endpoints, roles, totals, group ordering, stored metadata/counts/scores/actions and item ranking. Result: **zero mismatches in 153.811 seconds**. [Results](db_validate.json).
- Replayed retirement, malformed-object, missing-count, role-fallback, boolean-score, numeric and 1,001-Drug chunk fixtures. All subprocesses exited 0. An additional independent output-check script passed **19/19 assertions**. [Assertions](db_assertions.json), [checker](db_assertions.py).
- Current snapshot has 7 retired nodes, 142 retired edges, no self-loops and no live edges with retired endpoints. Its SPONSORS edges have 3,000 lead-sponsor and 1,652 collaborator roles; 43 originate at Investigator nodes. All six current node types are covered. [Graph facts](db_validate.json), [snapshot](db_snapshot_facts.json).
- All stored node/edge props are valid JSON objects. All 300 association scores are JSON real, all 3,176 sponsor counts are JSON integer, and all 3,000 stored node URLs start with `https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/`. Database SHA-256 remains `7341c5a7089731f67356da5a588b269d4b69e8e6c4c054acf19aa4ef2837b529`. [Snapshot script](db_snapshot.py), [facts](db_snapshot_facts.json).

+def post(path, text):
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    request = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=json.dumps({'text': text}).encode(),
+                                     headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
+    try:
+        response = urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=30)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
+        response = error
+    raw = response.read()
+    return {'path': path, 'text': text, 'characters': len(text), 'status': response.status,
+            'seconds': time.perf_counter() - start, 'bytes': len(raw), 'body': json.loads(raw)}

+            'AstraZeneca biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'osimertinib\nbiosimilar bevacizumab',
+        ]
+        cases = [post(path, value) for value in inputs
+                 for path in ['/api/explore', '/api/resolve']]
+        for value in [
+            'Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?',
+            'Which companies sponsor bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?',
+            'Which trials evaluate liposomal and nanoparticle formulations of doxorubicin?',
+            'Which trials evaluate liposomal and sustained release doxorubicin?',
+            'Which trials evaluate biosimilar and investigational version of bevacizumab?',
+            'Which trials evaluate Erlotinib, hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab?',
+            'Which trials evaluate Pegylated interferon alfa-2a?',
+            'Which trials evaluate PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A?',
+            'Which trials evaluate osimertinib and biosimilar bevacizumab?',
+            'Which companies sponsor osimertinib and biosimilar bevacizumab?',
+        ]:
+            cases.append(post('/api/ask', value))
+        (OUT / 'm5_http.json').write_text(json.dumps({'base': BASE, 'cases': cases},
+            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+        for case in cases:
+            body = case['body']
+            if case['path'] != '/api/resolve':
+                print(json.dumps({'text': case['text'], 'path': case['path'],
+                    'center': body.get('center', {}).get('id'), 'refused': body.get('refused'),
+                    'also': body.get('also'), 'evidence_count': len(body.get('evidence', [])),
+                    'first_edge': (body.get('evidence') or [{}])[0].get('edge_id')},
+                    ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)
+        slow = ' '.join(['x'] * 250)
+        sequential = []
+        for path, text in [('/api/explore', 'EGFR'), ('/api/explore', slow),
+                           ('/api/ask', slow), ('/api/explore', ' '.join(['x']*244+['osimertinib'])),
+                           ('/api/explore', ' '.join(['x']*251))]:
+            sequential.append(post(path, text))
+        # Four simultaneous allowed 499-character requests contend for the actual engine lock.
+        barrier = threading.Barrier(4)
+        def send_slow(_):
+            barrier.wait()
+            return post('/api/explore', slow)
+        started = time.perf_counter()
+        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
+            concurrent_rows = list(pool.map(send_slow, range(4)))
+        elapsed = time.perf_counter()-started
+        # Readable normal request behind one long lookup; .1 s is a measured scheduling gap,
+        # not a claim that a specific internal lock acquisition has been observed.
+        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
+            future = pool.submit(post, '/api/explore', slow)
+            time.sleep(.1)
+            follower = post('/api/explore', 'EGFR')
+            leader = future.result()
+        result = {'sequential': sequential, 'concurrent': concurrent_rows,
+                  'concurrent_wall_seconds': elapsed, 'normal_behind_slow': {'leader': leader, 'follower': follower},
+                  'normal_after': post('/api/explore', 'EGFR')}
+        (OUT / 'perf_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+        print(json.dumps({'performance_seconds': [r['seconds'] for r in sequential],
+                          'concurrent_seconds': [r['seconds'] for r in concurrent_rows],
+                          'concurrent_wall_seconds': elapsed, 'normal_behind_slow_seconds': follower['seconds']}))
+    finally:
+        proc.terminate()
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+        with socket.socket() as sock:
+            closed = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', PORT)) != 0
+        after = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+        (OUT / 'm5_http_lifecycle.json').write_text(json.dumps({'pid': proc.pid, 'port': PORT,
+            'server_exit': proc.returncode, 'port_closed': closed, 'database_unchanged': before == after,
+            'db_sha256_before': before, 'db_sha256_after': after}, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+        assert closed and before == after

+"""Bounded probe of the answer field passed back into the new linker."""
+import concurrent.futures
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import socket
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:8880'
+DB = ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite'
+
+def post(path, payload, timeout):
+    started = time.perf_counter()
+    try:
+        req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=json.dumps(payload).encode(),
+                                     headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
+        try:
+            response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout)
+        except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
+            response = error
+        return {'status': response.status, 'body': json.loads(response.read()),
+                'seconds': time.perf_counter()-started}
+    except TimeoutError:
+        return {'timeout_seconds': timeout, 'seconds': time.perf_counter()-started}
+
+with socket.socket() as sock:
+    assert sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', 8880)) != 0
+before = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1'}
+with (OUT / 'perf_check_server.txt').open('w', encoding='utf8') as log:
+    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-B', '-u', 'src/app.py', '8880'], cwd=ROOT,
+        env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0))
+    try:
+        for _ in range(100):
+            assert proc.poll() is None
+            try:
+                urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
+                break
+            except OSError:
+                time.sleep(.1)
+        else:
+            raise RuntimeError('server unavailable')
+        normal = post('/api/explore', {'text':'EGFR'}, 5)
+        small = post('/api/check', {'text':'EGFR', 'answer':' '.join(['x']*100)}, 5)
+        payload = {'text':'EGFR', 'answer':' '.join(['x']*500)}
+        slow = post('/api/check', payload, 5)
+        started = time.perf_counter()
+        get = urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=2)
+        get.read()
+        live_get = {'status': get.status, 'seconds': time.perf_counter()-started}
+        follower = post('/api/explore', {'text':'EGFR'}, 2)
+        output = {'normal_before': normal, 'small_check_199_characters': small,
+                  'slow_check_input': payload, 'slow_check_999_characters': slow,
+                  'get_while_check_running': live_get, 'normal_after_check_timeout': follower,
+                  'limitation':'Client timeouts bound the observation; not a completed latency or permanent deadlock claim.'}
+        (OUT / 'perf_check_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
+        print(json.dumps({k: ({x:y for x,y in v.items() if x!='body'} if isinstance(v,dict) else v)
+                          for k,v in output.items() if k!='slow_check_input'}))
+    finally:
+        proc.terminate()
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+        with socket.socket() as sock:
+            closed = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', 8880)) != 0
+        after = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+        (OUT / 'perf_check_lifecycle.json').write_text(json.dumps({'pid':proc.pid, 'port':8880,
+            'port_closed':closed,'database_unchanged':before==after,'server_exit':proc.returncode},indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+        assert closed and before==after

+"""Bounded subprocess benchmarks; writes confined to m5 and never calls a model."""
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+import types
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
+
+if len(sys.argv) > 1:
+    import query
+    import textrules as T
+    mode, kind, size = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3])
+    module = query
+    if mode == 'm4':
+        module = types.ModuleType('perf_m4_query')
+        module.__file__ = str(ROOT / 'src/query.py')
+        sys.modules[module.__name__] = module
+        code = subprocess.run(['git', 'show', 'bf436c6:src/query.py'], cwd=ROOT,
+                              capture_output=True, check=True).stdout.decode('utf-8')
+        exec(compile(code, module.__file__, 'exec'), module.__dict__)
+    kg = module.OncoKG()
+    kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
+    text = {'plain': ' '.join(['x'] * size),
+            'drug': ' '.join(['x'] * size + ['osimertinib']),
+            'split': ';'.join(['x'] * size),
+            'single': 'x' * size,
+            'late_qualifier': ' '.join(['x'] * size + ['biosimilar', 'bevacizumab'])}[kind]
+    calls = {'is_distinct_product': 0, 'is_radiotracer': 0, 'characters': 0}
+    for name in ['is_distinct_product', 'is_radiotracer']:
+        original = getattr(T, name)
+        def wrapped(value, name=name, original=original):
+            calls[name] += 1
+            calls['characters'] += len(value)
+            return original(value)
+        setattr(T, name, wrapped)
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    entities = kg.link_entities(text)
+    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
+    kg.conn.close()
+    print(json.dumps({'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'characters': len(text),
+                     'seconds': elapsed, 'calls': calls, 'entities': entities}))
+else:
+    env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1',
+           'PYTHONIOENCODING': 'utf-8', 'TEMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'),
+           'TMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'), 'TMPDIR': str(OUT / 'tmp')}
+    cases = [('plain', n) for n in [50, 100, 200, 250, 500, 1000]]
+    cases += [('drug', 244), ('split', 250), ('single', 500), ('late_qualifier', 235)]
+    rows = []
+    for kind, size in cases:
+        for mode in ['m4', 'HEAD']:
+            try:
+                proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-B', __file__, mode, kind, str(size)],
+                                      cwd=ROOT, env=env, capture_output=True, timeout=20)
+                row = json.loads(proc.stdout) if proc.returncode == 0 else {
+                    'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'error': proc.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace')}
+            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+                row = {'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'timeout_seconds': 20}
+            rows.append(row)
+            (OUT / 'perf_results.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+            print(json.dumps(row), flush=True)

| M4 target/company collateral refusal: `EGFR biosimilar bevacizumab`, `AstraZeneca biosimilar bevacizumab`, reverse/generic-version controls | **FIXED**. EGFR/AstraZeneca survive and refusal is disclosed. `names_attack.json`, `names_m5_attack.json`. |

## Blocker N1: missed attached separator defeats the product guard (inherited)

Exact input **`bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib`** returns centre **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, 122 trials, with OSIMERTINIB merely in `also`, and no refusal. `Engine.resolve` on the same exact input gives two components: `bevacizumab-awwb` **BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT**, `osimertinib` **EXACT / CHEMBL3353410**. The bare `bevacizumab-awwb` refuses in both current and preceding linker. This is a repository-internal counterexample to the refused-product guarantee, not an external interpretation of a drug name.

`Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?` returns 12 evidence rows beginning `NCT00955305|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1201583|NCT00955305`. `Which companies sponsor bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?` starts `ORG:roche|SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF|CHEMBL1201583|` (12 rows). These are the refused originator's evidence.

Variants `bevacizumab - awwb/ osimertinib`, `bevacizumab - awwb&osimertinib`, `bevacizumab - awwb& osimertinib` and `bevacizumab-awwb+ osimertinib` also expose BEVACIZUMAB. The resolver splits and refuses the product in each plain-string variant. A space on **both** sides of `/` instead triggers intended whole-item refusal in HEAD, because `/` is not a listed separator but the unadorned suffix becomes visible to the guard. A separated `&` is a recognized listing token and retains only osimertinib. The mismatch is tokenization-sensitive, and is also present in bf436c6.

Cause: `src/query.py:128-130` splits only five comma/semicolon characters and whitespace; `145-149` recognizes listing symbols only when they are the complete token. `/`, `+`, or `&` attached after the suffix stays in the guard's word. The anchored suffix predicate `src/textrules.py:156-157,213-223` then fails. Matching subsequently uses `canonical_key` (`src/query.py:182,184`; `src/textrules.py:339-346`) that discards that punctuation, recovering the stored originator alias after the refusal check has missed it. Exact current/predecessor/resolver/QA results: `names_m5_confirm.json`.

## Blocker N2: a listing word can split adjectives of one qualified product (inherited)

**`liposomal and investigational formulation of doxorubicin`** names one qualified formulation; HEAD refuses only `liposomal` and maps **DOXORUBICIN / CHEMBL53463**, 3 trials. The full-string product predicate is true. The unchanged full-string resolver returns `BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT`, no drug. `Which trials evaluate a liposomal and investigational formulation of doxorubicin?` returns all 3 doxorubicin trial rows, beginning `NCT01970540|EVALUATES|CHEMBL53463|NCT01970540`.

Additional exact cases `liposomal and nanoparticle formulations of doxorubicin` and `Which companies sponsor biosimilar and investigational versions of bevacizumab?` likewise recover the originator; the latter supplies 12 BEVACIZUMAB sponsor evidence rows. The singular-formulation example is the clearest: `and` coordinates descriptors, with no second named substance.

`_JOINERS` at `src/query.py:94-95` and the unconditional boundary `145-149` detach the qualifier before examining the drug. Item-local product scanning (`156-164`) cannot reconnect them. This follows the newly described implementation rule but still violates the unchanged product/QA approval guarantee. No claim is made that ordinary separate names such as `liposomal doxorubicin and osimertinib` should share qualifiers; that intended isolation succeeds. This bypass was also present in bf436c6. Evidence: `names_m5_confirm.json`.

## New cross-item alias regression: identities are stitched across the separators

**`Erlotinib, hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab`** should preserve first-item ERLOTINIB / CHEMBL553 and refuse the second item. Instead HEAD draws **ERLOTINIB HYDROCHLORIDE / CHEMBL1079742**, 31 trials, and simultaneously says `hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab` was refused. The exact preceding linker draws CHEMBL553 (118 trials). The current unchanged resolver also returns ERLOTINIB EXACT and the second item BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT. Independent controls `Erlotinib` and `hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab` yield the parent and refusal, respectively. The `and` variant preserves the parent. The semicolon variant repeats the new wrong-salt map.

`Which trials evaluate Erlotinib, hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab?` likewise changes the subject to CHEMBL1079742, returning 12 rows beginning `NCT00445848|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1079742|NCT00445848`.

This is an adversarial constructed input, not a claim that hydrochloride bevacizumab is a real marketed product. It tests the declared list contract and explicit salt-versus-parent distinction: two list items that independently mean parent plus refusal are combined into a third identity. It does **not** require external chemistry assumptions.

Cause: `_drug_spans(tokens, skip)` at `src/query.py:155,231-248` is given only joiner-token positions, with no segment IDs. It joins `Erlotinib` and `hydrochloride` across the comma. `165` assigns the entire span to **only `seg[a]`**, the first item's segment, so the later product qualifier does not mark any of those salt-span tokens refused. The final matcher (`174-184`) also has no segment-boundary guard, so it draws the synthetic salt name. Evidence: `names_m5_confirm.json`; full counterfactual setup in `names_m5_confirm.py`.

## Should-fix S1: a real drug alone in its own item is refused (new)

**`biosimilar anastrozole, Arimidex`** returns only `refused:'biosimilar anastrozole'`; Arimidex disappears. `Arimidex` by itself resolves/maps **ANASTROZOLE / CHEMBL1399**. `biosimilar anastrozole and Arimidex` retains it. `Engine.resolve` splits the comma input into a refused first component and Arimidex **ALIAS / CHEMBL1399**. bf436c6 also retained Arimidex. Trial QA for the comma input now has 0 evidence versus the `and` variant's 2 rows.

**`biosimilar Prolia, xgeva`** similarly refuses the entire input although Xgeva alone in the second item is an accepted alias for **DENOSUMAB / CHEMBL1237023**. `biosimilar recombinant, interleukin-2` likewise suppresses second-item ALDESLEUKIN.

This is explicitly within the user's should-fix exemption: the valid drug is **alone in its own comma-separated item**, not sharing an item with a qualifier. Cause is the same cross-clause span bug, in the other direction: the dictionary contains `anastrozole (arimidex)` / `Prolia, xgeva`; `_drug_spans` merges both item tokens, then `165-168` refuses the whole span when its **first** item has a qualifier. `names_m5_confirm.json` contains exact predecessor comparisons; `names_cross_item_refusal.json` has extra direct controls.

## Should-fix S2: intrinsic pattern in a valid lone alias (new map rejection, shared resolver limitation)

Stored ChEMBL synonym **`Pegylated interferon alfa-2a`** belongs uniquely to **CHEMBL1201560 / PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A** in the current dictionary. Used alone, HEAD refuses it and yields no map/QA evidence. The canonical name `PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A` and exact node ID both draw that same live molecule; canonical-name profile has 3 evidence entries. bf436c6 reached the same drug through the residual `alfa-2a` alias (with an incomplete refusal), so HEAD changes that alias's map from the correct stored entity to refusal.

The full resolver already refuses the synonym via `pegylated`; this is therefore **not** asserted to be newly inconsistent with the resolver. It is a dictionary/guard inconsistency and a lone-valid-alias usability issue under the explicit request to attack intrinsic qualifier patterns. There is no originator misidentification here, and no demo uses the synonym. Of 7,511 unambiguous alias keys, this was the only newly refused previously linked lone alias. The 50 alias anomaly rows include inherited refusals and benign refusal-label changes; they are not 50 new defects. Evidence: `names_m5_summary.json`, `names_m5_confirm.json`.

## Intended refusals and limits

Newlines, tabs, pipes, slash, Arabic comma/semicolon, halfwidth ideographic comma, full stops and phrases `as well as`, `together with`, `alongside` are not all recognized separators. When the ordinary `biosimilar` token is present, these usually cause both drugs to be refused. **Those ordinary same-item refusals are not findings** under the user's fail-closed rule. The attached-suffix bypass above is different: it returns the refused originator. Semicolon/fullwidth comma/ideographic comma and all explicit `_JOINERS` controls otherwise retain separately listed osimertinib.

Likewise `osimertinib with generic version of bevacizumab`, `osimertinib with [89Zr] trastuzumab`, and both drugs with a single shared-item qualifier intentionally refuse both. No should-fix is reported for these.

The complete old direct boundary script includes a >500-character API-independent call and took 27.196 s; this is not an HTTP timing. Root separately measures bounded HTTP inputs and long direct-input costs.

+## Blocking findings
+
+### M5-B1 — P1: attached separator hides a known refused suffix
+
+Exact unchanged-server request:
+
+```json
+{"text":"bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib"}
+```
+
+`POST /api/explore` returns HTTP 200 with **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, **122 trials**, OSIMERTINIB only in `also`, and **no refusal**. The linker reports `matched_text:'bevacizumab-awwb/'`, `matched_alias:'Bevacizumab awwb'`.
+
+The identical input at `/api/resolve` yields `bevacizumab-awwb: BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT` and `osimertinib: EXACT / CHEMBL3353410`. Bare `bevacizumab-awwb` is refused. Thus the test names a product the repository itself refuses; no external pharmacology assumption is needed. [Actual HTTP responses](m5_http.json), [exact current/predecessor linkage](names_m5_confirm.json).
+
+| Exact `/api/ask` text | Current evidence |
+|---|---|
+| `Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?` | 12 rows, first `NCT00955305\|EVALUATES\|CHEMBL1201583\|NCT00955305` |
+| `Which companies sponsor bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?` | 12 rows, first `ORG:roche\|SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF\|CHEMBL1201583\|` |
+
+**Cause:** the splitter at `src/query.py:128-130` leaves `/` attached to the word; listing symbols are recognized only as whole tokens (`145-149`). `RE_BIOSIMILAR_SUFFIX` is anchored to the end of the string (`src/textrules.py:156-157,213-223`), so the extra slash defeats it. No contiguous *word* run removes that slash. Alias matching then calls `canonical_key`, which discards punctuation (`src/textrules.py:339-346`; `src/query.py:182-197`), recovering the originator alias after the guard missed it. `Engine.explore` trusts that hit (`src/app.py:88-95`).
+
+`bevacizumab - awwb/ osimertinib` and `bevacizumab-awwb| osimertinib` also leak over HTTP. With spaces on both sides of `/`, the guard recognizes the suffix and HEAD refuses the entire item; semicolon or `and` correctly preserves only osimertinib. The whole-item slash refusal is accepted under the user's rule, not counted as over-refusal. Exact `bf436c6` comparison reproduces the attached-slash leak: **inherited, not introduced by HEAD**.
+
+**Required outcome:** punctuation that alias normalization removes must not hide a refusal. Never map or answer for CHEMBL1201583 from this refused component. Retaining the separately recognized osimertinib or refusing an ambiguous combined item can both be safe outcomes; selecting BEVACIZUMAB cannot.
+
+### M5-B2 — P1: a listing word inside one product description detaches its qualifier
+
+Exact input **`liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin`** returns HTTP 200 with **DOXORUBICIN / CHEMBL53463**, **3 trials**, and `also:[{"refused":"liposomal"}]`. The full-string resolver returns a single **BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT** component with no accepted drug. `and` joins descriptions of one formulation here; it does not introduce another named drug. [HTTP](m5_http.json), [independent reproduction](ui_boundary_probe.json).
+
+`Which trials evaluate liposomal and nanoparticle formulations of doxorubicin?` returns **3 originator trial rows**, beginning `NCT01970540|EVALUATES|CHEMBL53463|NCT01970540`. The singular QA version independently yields the same three rows. The singular/plural map forms and `liposomal and sustained release doxorubicin` also fail. [Actual QA](m5_http.json), [independent singular QA](ui_boundary_probe.json).
+
+**Cause:** `_JOINERS` (`src/query.py:94-95`) and the unconditional boundary at `146-149` isolate `liposomal` before examining the drug. The scan at `156-164` only considers words within one resulting item; nothing in the second item matches the current refusal patterns, so DOXORUBICIN remains a positive hit (`190-198`). The full product predicate recognizes the original phrase via `liposomal` (`src/textrules.py:148,207-208`). The fail-closed rule cannot protect a qualifier the splitter has already detached.
+
+This is a different mechanism from the attached-punctuation bypass. It also occurs with the exact previous linker, so it is **inherited**, now confirmed against the changed design. Ordinary `liposomal doxorubicin and osimertinib` is not the requested failure case: separate named products should retain their isolation. No over-refusal of a valid same-item drug is alleged here.
+
+**Required outcome:** a conjunction inside a single qualified product must not make its originator acceptable. If such a phrase cannot be resolved safely, refuse its drug. Preserve the successful separate-drug mixed-input controls.
+
+## Should-fix findings, separate from the approval blockers
+
+### M5-S1 — P2: alias matching crosses the very list boundaries used for refusal
+
+Two complementary effects share a cause:
+
+1. **A valid drug alone in its own item disappears.** `biosimilar anastrozole, Arimidex` returns only `refused:'biosimilar anastrozole'`. Its second item is a valid standalone alias: `Arimidex` and `biosimilar anastrozole and Arimidex` both select **ANASTROZOLE / CHEMBL1399**. The resolver gives a refused first component and `Arimidex: ALIAS / CHEMBL1399`; the exact predecessor retains Arimidex. Current comma QA has **0** evidence versus standalone Arimidex's **2** rows. `biosimilar Prolia, xgeva` similarly loses the separately listed Xgeva, while Xgeva alone returns DENOSUMAB / CHEMBL1237023 and **3** trial rows. These are actual CLI HTTP reproductions. [HTTP](cross_item_http.json), [prior/current comparison](names_m5_confirm.json), [extra controls](names_cross_item_refusal.json).
+2. **A different salt identity is assembled from two items.** `Erlotinib, hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab` now selects **ERLOTINIB HYDROCHLORIDE / CHEMBL1079742**, **31 trials**, while declaring the second item refused. The exact predecessor and current full resolver preserve first-item **ERLOTINIB / CHEMBL553**, **118 trials**. The semicolon form repeats the wrong-salt selection; the `and` form preserves the parent. Trial QA returns 12 salt rows, first `NCT00445848|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1079742|NCT00445848`. This is a constructed list-contract test, not a claim that hydrochloride bevacizumab is a marketed product. [HTTP](m5_http.json), [comparison](names_m5_confirm.json).
+
+`_drug_spans` receives only listing-word positions to skip (`src/query.py:155`); it has no clause/segment boundaries (`231-248`). Aggressive keys join `anastrozole Arimidex` into the stored `anastrozole (arimidex)` alias, or `Erlotinib hydrochloride` across a comma. Line **165** assigns the entire span using only `seg[a]`. A qualifier in its first item refuses tokens in the second; a qualifier in its second item misses the span assigned to the first. The final positive matcher also joins windows without segment checks (`174-198`). This is **new versus bf436c6** for the cited inputs.
+
+The Arimidex/Xgeva cases satisfy the user's explicit should-fix exception: the valid alias is **alone in its own item**. They are not ordinary intended same-item over-refusals. Keep both refusal discovery and accepted-name matching within the same item boundaries; do not borrow words from a refused item to build another identity.
+
+### M5-S2 — P2: an intrinsic qualifier rejects a valid lone stored alias
+
+**`Pegylated interferon alfa-2a`** is a unique stored ChEMBL synonym of **CHEMBL1201560 / PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A**. Used alone, HEAD refuses it. The canonical name `PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A` and exact node ID both draw that live drug. The previous linker reached the same drug through residual `alfa-2a`, accompanied by an incomplete refusal; HEAD now produces no map. Of 7,511 unambiguous alias keys, this is the only newly refused previously linked lone alias. [Stored alias/comparison](names_m5_summary.json), [HTTP controls](m5_http.json).
+
+The full resolver already blocks the synonym via `pegylated`, so this is a **shared dictionary/guard inconsistency**, not a new resolver disagreement. The alias names the stored pegylated drug itself; no originator substitution is demonstrated. It is should-fix under the explicit lone-valid-alias test, and does not block a demo. Do not generalize this into accepting every known alias before product guards: the B1 suffix alias shows why such an exemption would be unsafe. Relevant logic: `src/textrules.py:148,207-208`; `src/query.py:153-168`.
+
+### M5-S3 — P2: all-substring refusal scan creates substantial input cost under the global lock
+
+For an unqualified item containing n tokens, `src/query.py:161-163` tries n(n+1)/2 word runs. Constructing and scanning their strings incurs cubic aggregate character work for equal-length tokens. The scan runs even without any drug. Instrumented exact-predecessor/current runs use the same inputs/data; times are local observations, not universal performance guarantees. [Reproducer](perf_probe.py), [results](perf_results.json).
+
+| Exact constructed input | Characters | bf436c6 linker | HEAD linker |
+|---|---:|---:|---:|
+| `" ".join(["x"] * 50)` | 99 | 0.0012 s | 0.0220 s |
+| `" ".join(["x"] * 100)` | 199 | 0.0022 s | 0.1332 s |
+| `" ".join(["x"] * 200)` | 399 | 0.0046 s | 0.9778 s |
+| `" ".join(["x"] * 250)` | 499 | 0.0064 s | 1.7907 s |
+| `" ".join(["x"] * 500)` — direct API | 999 | 0.0116 s | 18.3512 s |
+| `" ".join(["x"] * 1000)` — direct API | 1,999 | 0.0249 s | worker exceeded 20 s; stopped |
+
+At 250 tokens HEAD makes **31,625 calls to each predicate**, processing **10,479,750 aggregate characters** across the two predicates, versus 250 calls each / 500 characters in the predecessor. A single 500-character word takes 0.0073 s; 250 semicolon-separated x items take 0.0102 s; a late biosimilar qualifier short-circuits at 0.0225 s. This is token/run growth, not simply request byte length.
+
+Uninstrumented HTTP confirmation: the allowed 499-character input took **1.8472 s** in `/api/explore`, **2.2104 s** in `/api/ask`; adding a valid osimertinib within 499 characters took **2.1657 s**. Four simultaneous allowed inputs completed in **7.9315 s**, with individual latencies up to **7.9215 s**. A normal EGFR request dispatched 0.1 s after one long lookup took **1.8568 s**, versus **0.0366 s** after it completed. The actual server holds `engine.lock` during linking (`src/app.py:247-263`). A 501-character text is promptly rejected, as intended. [Raw HTTP measurements](perf_http.json).
+
+The 500-character text cap does not bound every caller: `/api/check` allows a 4,000-character answer (`src/app.py:260`), which is relinked by `check_groundedness` (`src/query.py:605-608`). With `{"text":"EGFR","answer":" ".join(["x"]*500)}`, the 999-character answer exceeded a **5-second** client timeout; the following ordinary EGFR map exceeded its **2-second** timeout while GET `/` still succeeded. The owned process was then terminated. These are bounded observations, **not a completed latency or permanent-deadlock claim**. [Exact payload/results](perf_check_http.json), [cleanup](perf_check_lifecycle.json).
+
+Preserve refusal coverage while avoiding exhaustive reconstruction of every word run, and cover the answer-field caller when bounding work. This is should-fix performance evidence, separate from originator leaks.
+

+"""Round-5 independent HTTP confirmations and bounded latency/lock measurements."""
+import concurrent.futures
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import socket
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import threading
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+PORT = 8880
+BASE = f'http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}'
+DB = ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite'
+
+def post(path, text):
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    request = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=json.dumps({'text': text}).encode(),
+                                     headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
+    try:
+        response = urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=30)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
+        response = error
+    raw = response.read()
+    return {'path': path, 'text': text, 'characters': len(text), 'status': response.status,
+            'seconds': time.perf_counter() - start, 'bytes': len(raw), 'body': json.loads(raw)}
+
+with socket.socket() as sock:
+    assert sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', PORT)) != 0, 'audit port occupied'
+before = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1',
+       'PYTHONIOENCODING': 'utf-8', 'TEMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'), 'TMP': str(OUT / 'tmp')}
+with (OUT / 'm5_server.txt').open('w', encoding='utf-8') as log:
+    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-B', '-u', 'src/app.py', str(PORT)],
+                            cwd=ROOT, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+                            creationflags=getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0))
+    try:
+        for _ in range(100):
+            assert proc.poll() is None
+            try:
+                urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
+                break
+            except OSError:
+                time.sleep(.1)
+        else:
+            raise RuntimeError('server unavailable')
+        inputs = [
+            'bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab - awwb/ osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab-awwb / osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab - awwb / osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab-awwb| osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab-awwb; osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab-awwb and osimertinib',
+            'liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin',
+            'liposomal and nanoparticle formulations of doxorubicin',
+            'liposomal and sustained release doxorubicin',
+            'biosimilar and investigational version of bevacizumab',
+            'Erlotinib, hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'Erlotinib; hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'Erlotinib and hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'Erlotinib hydrochloride', 'erlotinib',
+            'Pegylated interferon alfa-2a', 'PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A',
+            'CHEMBL1201560', 'EGFR biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'AstraZeneca biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'osimertinib\nbiosimilar bevacizumab',
+        ]
+        cases = [post(path, value) for value in inputs
+                 for path in ['/api/explore', '/api/resolve']]
+        for value in [
+            'Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?',
+            'Which companies sponsor bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?',
+            'Which trials evaluate liposomal and nanoparticle formulations of doxorubicin?',
+            'Which trials evaluate liposomal and sustained release doxorubicin?',
+            'Which trials evaluate biosimilar and investigational version of bevacizumab?',
+            'Which trials evaluate Erlotinib, hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab?',
+            'Which trials evaluate Pegylated interferon alfa-2a?',
+            'Which trials evaluate PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A?',
+            'Which trials evaluate osimertinib and biosimilar bevacizumab?',
+            'Which companies sponsor osimertinib and biosimilar bevacizumab?',
+        ]:
+            cases.append(post('/api/ask', value))
+        (OUT / 'm5_http.json').write_text(json.dumps({'base': BASE, 'cases': cases},
+            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+        for case in cases:
+            body = case['body']
+            if case['path'] != '/api/resolve':
+                print(json.dumps({'text': case['text'], 'path': case['path'],
+                    'center': body.get('center', {}).get('id'), 'refused': body.get('refused'),
+                    'also': body.get('also'), 'evidence_count': len(body.get('evidence', [])),
+                    'first_edge': (body.get('evidence') or [{}])[0].get('edge_id')},
+                    ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)
+        slow = ' '.join(['x'] * 250)
+        sequential = []
+        for path, text in [('/api/explore', 'EGFR'), ('/api/explore', slow),
+                           ('/api/ask', slow), ('/api/explore', ' '.join(['x']*244+['osimertinib'])),
+                           ('/api/explore', ' '.join(['x']*251))]:
+            sequential.append(post(path, text))
+        # Four simultaneous allowed 499-character requests contend for the actual engine lock.
+        barrier = threading.Barrier(4)
+        def send_slow(_):
+            barrier.wait()
+            return post('/api/explore', slow)
+        started = time.perf_counter()
+        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
+            concurrent_rows = list(pool.map(send_slow, range(4)))
+        elapsed = time.perf_counter()-started
+        # Readable normal request behind one long lookup; .1 s is a measured scheduling gap,
+        # not a claim that a specific internal lock acquisition has been observed.
+        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
+            future = pool.submit(post, '/api/explore', slow)
+            time.sleep(.1)
+            follower = post('/api/explore', 'EGFR')
+            leader = future.result()
+        result = {'sequential': sequential, 'concurrent': concurrent_rows,
+                  'concurrent_wall_seconds': elapsed, 'normal_behind_slow': {'leader': leader, 'follower': follower},
+                  'normal_after': post('/api/explore', 'EGFR')}
+        (OUT / 'perf_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+        print(json.dumps({'performance_seconds': [r['seconds'] for r in sequential],
+                          'concurrent_seconds': [r['seconds'] for r in concurrent_rows],
+                          'concurrent_wall_seconds': elapsed, 'normal_behind_slow_seconds': follower['seconds']}))
+    finally:
+        proc.terminate()
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+        with socket.socket() as sock:
+            closed = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', PORT)) != 0
+        after = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+        (OUT / 'm5_http_lifecycle.json').write_text(json.dumps({'pid': proc.pid, 'port': PORT,
+            'server_exit': proc.returncode, 'port_closed': closed, 'database_unchanged': before == after,
+            'db_sha256_before': before, 'db_sha256_after': after}, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+        assert closed and before == after

+"""Bounded probe of the answer field passed back into the new linker."""
+import concurrent.futures
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import socket
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:8880'
+DB = ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite'
+
+def post(path, payload, timeout):
+    started = time.perf_counter()
+    try:
+        req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=json.dumps(payload).encode(),
+                                     headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
+        try:
+            response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout)
+        except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
+            response = error
+        return {'status': response.status, 'body': json.loads(response.read()),
+                'seconds': time.perf_counter()-started}
+    except TimeoutError:
+        return {'timeout_seconds': timeout, 'seconds': time.perf_counter()-started}
+
+with socket.socket() as sock:
+    assert sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', 8880)) != 0
+before = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1'}
+with (OUT / 'perf_check_server.txt').open('w', encoding='utf8') as log:
+    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-B', '-u', 'src/app.py', '8880'], cwd=ROOT,
+        env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0))
+    try:
+        for _ in range(100):
+            assert proc.poll() is None
+            try:
+                urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
+                break
+            except OSError:
+                time.sleep(.1)
+        else:
+            raise RuntimeError('server unavailable')
+        normal = post('/api/explore', {'text':'EGFR'}, 5)
+        small = post('/api/check', {'text':'EGFR', 'answer':' '.join(['x']*100)}, 5)
+        payload = {'text':'EGFR', 'answer':' '.join(['x']*500)}
+        slow = post('/api/check', payload, 5)
+        started = time.perf_counter()
+        get = urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=2)
+        get.read()
+        live_get = {'status': get.status, 'seconds': time.perf_counter()-started}
+        follower = post('/api/explore', {'text':'EGFR'}, 2)
+        output = {'normal_before': normal, 'small_check_199_characters': small,
+                  'slow_check_input': payload, 'slow_check_999_characters': slow,
+                  'get_while_check_running': live_get, 'normal_after_check_timeout': follower,
+                  'limitation':'Client timeouts bound the observation; not a completed latency or permanent deadlock claim.'}
+        (OUT / 'perf_check_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
+        print(json.dumps({k: ({x:y for x,y in v.items() if x!='body'} if isinstance(v,dict) else v)
+                          for k,v in output.items() if k!='slow_check_input'}))
+    finally:
+        proc.terminate()
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+        with socket.socket() as sock:
+            closed = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', 8880)) != 0
+        after = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+        (OUT / 'perf_check_lifecycle.json').write_text(json.dumps({'pid':proc.pid, 'port':8880,
+            'port_closed':closed,'database_unchanged':before==after,'server_exit':proc.returncode},indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+        assert closed and before==after

+"""Bounded subprocess benchmarks; writes confined to m5 and never calls a model."""
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+import types
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
+
+if len(sys.argv) > 1:
+    import query
+    import textrules as T
+    mode, kind, size = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3])
+    module = query
+    if mode == 'm4':
+        module = types.ModuleType('perf_m4_query')
+        module.__file__ = str(ROOT / 'src/query.py')
+        sys.modules[module.__name__] = module
+        code = subprocess.run(['git', 'show', 'bf436c6:src/query.py'], cwd=ROOT,
+                              capture_output=True, check=True).stdout.decode('utf-8')
+        exec(compile(code, module.__file__, 'exec'), module.__dict__)
+    kg = module.OncoKG()
+    kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
+    text = {'plain': ' '.join(['x'] * size),
+            'drug': ' '.join(['x'] * size + ['osimertinib']),
+            'split': ';'.join(['x'] * size),
+            'single': 'x' * size,
+            'late_qualifier': ' '.join(['x'] * size + ['biosimilar', 'bevacizumab'])}[kind]
+    calls = {'is_distinct_product': 0, 'is_radiotracer': 0, 'characters': 0}
+    for name in ['is_distinct_product', 'is_radiotracer']:
+        original = getattr(T, name)
+        def wrapped(value, name=name, original=original):
+            calls[name] += 1
+            calls['characters'] += len(value)
+            return original(value)
+        setattr(T, name, wrapped)
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    entities = kg.link_entities(text)
+    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
+    kg.conn.close()
+    print(json.dumps({'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'characters': len(text),
+                     'seconds': elapsed, 'calls': calls, 'entities': entities}))
+else:
+    env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1',
+           'PYTHONIOENCODING': 'utf-8', 'TEMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'),
+           'TMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'), 'TMPDIR': str(OUT / 'tmp')}
+    cases = [('plain', n) for n in [50, 100, 200, 250, 500, 1000]]
+    cases += [('drug', 244), ('split', 250), ('single', 500), ('late_qualifier', 235)]
+    rows = []
+    for kind, size in cases:
+        for mode in ['m4', 'HEAD']:
+            try:
+                proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-B', __file__, mode, kind, str(size)],
+                                      cwd=ROOT, env=env, capture_output=True, timeout=20)
+                row = json.loads(proc.stdout) if proc.returncode == 0 else {
+                    'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'error': proc.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace')}
+            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+                row = {'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'timeout_seconds': 20}
+            rows.append(row)
+            (OUT / 'perf_results.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+            print(json.dumps(row), flush=True)

+# Independent relation-map review — round 5
+
+Reviewed HEAD: `514929c83fb7ab8cdd23fdd50eb300bd409c3250`, 2026-11-03. Immediate predecessor: `bf436c6`; cumulative repair comparison: `git diff bfcb83d...HEAD`. Specification: the user's round-5 brief, original approval bar, [m1](../m1/REPORT.md), [m2b](../m2b/REPORT.md), [m3](../m3/REPORT.md), [m4](../m4/REPORT.md), and `CODEX_MAP_M1_VERDICT.md`.
+
+**Approval remains blocked by two independently reproduced product-refusal bypasses.** An attached separator defeats a suffix refusal; a listing word can detach a formulation qualifier from its sole drug. Both draw the refused product's originator and return that originator's QA evidence. Exact predecessor comparisons show both are inherited bypasses, newly identified here, rather than newly introduced by this commit. All previously named blockers are FIXED for their exact inputs under the revised fail-closed policy.
+
+The new implementation also merges drug aliases across its own comma/semicolon boundaries. This can select the wrong salt or suppress a valid drug alone in another item. Those findings, a lone-alias rejection, and input cost are separated from the decisive product-refusal blockers below. Ordinary over-refusal of drugs sharing one item is **accepted behavior**, not a finding.
+
+## Verification, evidence and limits
+

+
+## Blocking findings
+
+### M5-B1 — P1: attached separator hides a known refused suffix
+
+Exact unchanged-server request:
+
+```json
+{"text":"bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib"}
+```
+
+`POST /api/explore` returns HTTP 200 with **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, **122 trials**, OSIMERTINIB only in `also`, and **no refusal**. The linker reports `matched_text:'bevacizumab-awwb/'`, `matched_alias:'Bevacizumab awwb'`.
+
+The identical input at `/api/resolve` yields `bevacizumab-awwb: BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT` and `osimertinib: EXACT / CHEMBL3353410`. Bare `bevacizumab-awwb` is refused. Thus the test names a product the repository itself refuses; no external pharmacology assumption is needed. [Actual HTTP responses](m5_http.json), [exact current/predecessor linkage](names_m5_confirm.json).
+
+| Exact `/api/ask` text | Current evidence |
+|---|---|
+| `Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?` | 12 rows, first `NCT00955305\|EVALUATES\|CHEMBL1201583\|NCT00955305` |
+| `Which companies sponsor bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?` | 12 rows, first `ORG:roche\|SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF\|CHEMBL1201583\|` |
+
+**Cause:** the splitter at `src/query.py:128-130` leaves `/` attached to the word; listing symbols are recognized only as whole tokens (`145-149`). `RE_BIOSIMILAR_SUFFIX` is anchored to the end of the string (`src/textrules.py:156-157,213-223`), so the extra slash defeats it. No contiguous *word* run removes that slash. Alias matching then calls `canonical_key`, which discards punctuation (`src/textrules.py:339-346`; `src/query.py:182-197`), recovering the originator alias after the guard missed it. `Engine.explore` trusts that hit (`src/app.py:88-95`).
+
+`bevacizumab - awwb/ osimertinib` and `bevacizumab-awwb| osimertinib` also leak over HTTP. With spaces on both sides of `/`, the guard recognizes the suffix and HEAD refuses the entire item; semicolon or `and` correctly preserves only osimertinib. The whole-item slash refusal is accepted under the user's rule, not counted as over-refusal. Exact `bf436c6` comparison reproduces the attached-slash leak: **inherited, not introduced by HEAD**.
+
+**Required outcome:** punctuation that alias normalization removes must not hide a refusal. Never map or answer for CHEMBL1201583 from this refused component. Retaining the separately recognized osimertinib or refusing an ambiguous combined item can both be safe outcomes; selecting BEVACIZUMAB cannot.
+
+### M5-B2 — P1: a listing word inside one product description detaches its qualifier
+
+Exact input **`liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin`** returns HTTP 200 with **DOXORUBICIN / CHEMBL53463**, **3 trials**, and `also:[{"refused":"liposomal"}]`. The full-string resolver returns a single **BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT** component with no accepted drug. `and` joins descriptions of one formulation here; it does not introduce another named drug. [HTTP](m5_http.json), [independent reproduction](ui_boundary_probe.json).
+
+`Which trials evaluate liposomal and nanoparticle formulations of doxorubicin?` returns **3 originator trial rows**, beginning `NCT01970540|EVALUATES|CHEMBL53463|NCT01970540`. The singular QA version independently yields the same three rows. The singular/plural map forms and `liposomal and sustained release doxorubicin` also fail. [Actual QA](m5_http.json), [independent singular QA](ui_boundary_probe.json).
+
+**Cause:** `_JOINERS` (`src/query.py:94-95`) and the unconditional boundary at `146-149` isolate `liposomal` before examining the drug. The scan at `156-164` only considers words within one resulting item; nothing in the second item matches the current refusal patterns, so DOXORUBICIN remains a positive hit (`190-198`). The full product predicate recognizes the original phrase via `liposomal` (`src/textrules.py:148,207-208`). The fail-closed rule cannot protect a qualifier the splitter has already detached.
+
+This is a different mechanism from the attached-punctuation bypass. It also occurs with the exact previous linker, so it is **inherited**, now confirmed against the changed design. Ordinary `liposomal doxorubicin and osimertinib` is not the requested failure case: separate named products should retain their isolation. No over-refusal of a valid same-item drug is alleged here.
+
+**Required outcome:** a conjunction inside a single qualified product must not make its originator acceptable. If such a phrase cannot be resolved safely, refuse its drug. Preserve the successful separate-drug mixed-input controls.
+
+## Should-fix findings, separate from the approval blockers
+
+### M5-S1 — P2: alias matching crosses the very list boundaries used for refusal
+
+Two complementary effects share a cause:
+
+1. **A valid drug alone in its own item disappears.** `biosimilar anastrozole, Arimidex` returns only `refused:'biosimilar anastrozole'`. Its second item is a valid standalone alias: `Arimidex` and `biosimilar anastrozole and Arimidex` both select **ANASTROZOLE / CHEMBL1399**. The resolver gives a refused first component and `Arimidex: ALIAS / CHEMBL1399`; the exact predecessor retains Arimidex. Current comma QA has **0** evidence versus standalone Arimidex's **2** rows. `biosimilar Prolia, xgeva` similarly loses the separately listed Xgeva, while Xgeva alone returns DENOSUMAB / CHEMBL1237023 and **3** trial rows. These are actual CLI HTTP reproductions. [HTTP](cross_item_http.json), [prior/current comparison](names_m5_confirm.json), [extra controls](names_cross_item_refusal.json).
+2. **A different salt identity is assembled from two items.** `Erlotinib, hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab` now selects **ERLOTINIB HYDROCHLORIDE / CHEMBL1079742**, **31 trials**, while declaring the second item refused. The exact predecessor and current full resolver preserve first-item **ERLOTINIB / CHEMBL553**, **118 trials**. The semicolon form repeats the wrong-salt selection; the `and` form preserves the parent. Trial QA returns 12 salt rows, first `NCT00445848|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1079742|NCT00445848`. This is a constructed list-contract test, not a claim that hydrochloride bevacizumab is a marketed product. [HTTP](m5_http.json), [comparison](names_m5_confirm.json).
+
+`_drug_spans` receives only listing-word positions to skip (`src/query.py:155`); it has no clause/segment boundaries (`231-248`). Aggressive keys join `anastrozole Arimidex` into the stored `anastrozole (arimidex)` alias, or `Erlotinib hydrochloride` across a comma. Line **165** assigns the entire span using only `seg[a]`. A qualifier in its first item refuses tokens in the second; a qualifier in its second item misses the span assigned to the first. The final positive matcher also joins windows without segment checks (`174-198`). This is **new versus bf436c6** for the cited inputs.
+
+The Arimidex/Xgeva cases satisfy the user's explicit should-fix exception: the valid alias is **alone in its own item**. They are not ordinary intended same-item over-refusals. Keep both refusal discovery and accepted-name matching within the same item boundaries; do not borrow words from a refused item to build another identity.
+
+### M5-S2 — P2: an intrinsic qualifier rejects a valid lone stored alias
+
+**`Pegylated interferon alfa-2a`** is a unique stored ChEMBL synonym of **CHEMBL1201560 / PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A**. Used alone, HEAD refuses it. The canonical name `PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A` and exact node ID both draw that live drug. The previous linker reached the same drug through residual `alfa-2a`, accompanied by an incomplete refusal; HEAD now produces no map. Of 7,511 unambiguous alias keys, this is the only newly refused previously linked lone alias. [Stored alias/comparison](names_m5_summary.json), [HTTP controls](m5_http.json).
+
+The full resolver already blocks the synonym via `pegylated`, so this is a **shared dictionary/guard inconsistency**, not a new resolver disagreement. The alias names the stored pegylated drug itself; no originator substitution is demonstrated. It is should-fix under the explicit lone-valid-alias test, and does not block a demo. Do not generalize this into accepting every known alias before product guards: the B1 suffix alias shows why such an exemption would be unsafe. Relevant logic: `src/textrules.py:148,207-208`; `src/query.py:153-168`.
+
+### M5-S3 — P2: all-substring refusal scan creates substantial input cost under the global lock
+
+For an unqualified item containing n tokens, `src/query.py:161-163` tries n(n+1)/2 word runs. Constructing and scanning their strings incurs cubic aggregate character work for equal-length tokens. The scan runs even without any drug. Instrumented exact-predecessor/current runs use the same inputs/data; times are local observations, not universal performance guarantees. [Reproducer](perf_probe.py), [results](perf_results.json).
+
+| Exact constructed input | Characters | bf436c6 linker | HEAD linker |
+|---|---:|---:|---:|
+| `" ".join(["x"] * 50)` | 99 | 0.0012 s | 0.0220 s |
+| `" ".join(["x"] * 100)` | 199 | 0.0022 s | 0.1332 s |
+| `" ".join(["x"] * 200)` | 399 | 0.0046 s | 0.9778 s |
+| `" ".join(["x"] * 250)` | 499 | 0.0064 s | 1.7907 s |
+| `" ".join(["x"] * 500)` — direct API | 999 | 0.0116 s | 18.3512 s |
+| `" ".join(["x"] * 1000)` — direct API | 1,999 | 0.0249 s | worker exceeded 20 s; stopped |
+
+At 250 tokens HEAD makes **31,625 calls to each predicate**, processing **10,479,750 aggregate characters** across the two predicates, versus 250 calls each / 500 characters in the predecessor. A single 500-character word takes 0.0073 s; 250 semicolon-separated x items take 0.0102 s; a late biosimilar qualifier short-circuits at 0.0225 s. This is token/run growth, not simply request byte length.
+
+Uninstrumented HTTP confirmation: the allowed 499-character input took **1.8472 s** in `/api/explore`, **2.2104 s** in `/api/ask`; adding a valid osimertinib within 499 characters took **2.1657 s**. Four simultaneous allowed inputs completed in **7.9315 s**, with individual latencies up to **7.9215 s**. A normal EGFR request dispatched 0.1 s after one long lookup took **1.8568 s**, versus **0.0366 s** after it completed. The actual server holds `engine.lock` during linking (`src/app.py:247-263`). A 501-character text is promptly rejected, as intended. [Raw HTTP measurements](perf_http.json).
+
+The 500-character text cap does not bound every caller: `/api/check` allows a 4,000-character answer (`src/app.py:260`), which is relinked by `check_groundedness` (`src/query.py:605-608`). With `{"text":"EGFR","answer":" ".join(["x"]*500)}`, the 999-character answer exceeded a **5-second** client timeout; the following ordinary EGFR map exceeded its **2-second** timeout while GET `/` still succeeded. The owned process was then terminated. These are bounded observations, **not a completed latency or permanent-deadlock claim**. [Exact payload/results](perf_check_http.json), [cleanup](perf_check_lifecycle.json).
+
+Preserve refusal coverage while avoiding exhaustive reconstruction of every word run, and cover the answer-field caller when bounding work. This is should-fix performance evidence, separate from originator leaks.
+
