+"""Round-5 independent HTTP confirmations and bounded latency/lock measurements."""
+import concurrent.futures
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import socket
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import threading
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+PORT = 8880
+BASE = f'http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}'
+DB = ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite'
+
+def post(path, text):
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    request = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=json.dumps({'text': text}).encode(),
+                                     headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
+    try:
+        response = urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=30)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
+        response = error
+    raw = response.read()
+    return {'path': path, 'text': text, 'characters': len(text), 'status': response.status,
+            'seconds': time.perf_counter() - start, 'bytes': len(raw), 'body': json.loads(raw)}
+
+with socket.socket() as sock:
+    assert sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', PORT)) != 0, 'audit port occupied'
+before = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1',
+       'PYTHONIOENCODING': 'utf-8', 'TEMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'), 'TMP': str(OUT / 'tmp')}
+with (OUT / 'm5_server.txt').open('w', encoding='utf-8') as log:
+    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-B', '-u', 'src/app.py', str(PORT)],
+                            cwd=ROOT, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+                            creationflags=getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0))
+    try:
+        for _ in range(100):
+            assert proc.poll() is None
+            try:
+                urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
+                break
+            except OSError:
+                time.sleep(.1)
+        else:
+            raise RuntimeError('server unavailable')
+        inputs = [
+            'bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab - awwb/ osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab-awwb / osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab - awwb / osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab-awwb| osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab-awwb; osimertinib',
+            'bevacizumab-awwb and osimertinib',
+            'liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin',
+            'liposomal and nanoparticle formulations of doxorubicin',
+            'liposomal and sustained release doxorubicin',
+            'biosimilar and investigational version of bevacizumab',
+            'Erlotinib, hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'Erlotinib; hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'Erlotinib and hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'Erlotinib hydrochloride', 'erlotinib',
+            'Pegylated interferon alfa-2a', 'PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A',
+            'CHEMBL1201560', 'EGFR biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'AstraZeneca biosimilar bevacizumab',
+            'osimertinib\nbiosimilar bevacizumab',
+        ]
+        cases = [post(path, value) for value in inputs
+                 for path in ['/api/explore', '/api/resolve']]
+        for value in [
+            'Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?',
+            'Which companies sponsor bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?',
+            'Which trials evaluate liposomal and nanoparticle formulations of doxorubicin?',
+            'Which trials evaluate liposomal and sustained release doxorubicin?',
+            'Which trials evaluate biosimilar and investigational version of bevacizumab?',
+            'Which trials evaluate Erlotinib, hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab?',
+            'Which trials evaluate Pegylated interferon alfa-2a?',
+            'Which trials evaluate PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A?',
+            'Which trials evaluate osimertinib and biosimilar bevacizumab?',
+            'Which companies sponsor osimertinib and biosimilar bevacizumab?',
+        ]:
+            cases.append(post('/api/ask', value))
+        (OUT / 'm5_http.json').write_text(json.dumps({'base': BASE, 'cases': cases},
+            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+        for case in cases:
+            body = case['body']
+            if case['path'] != '/api/resolve':
+                print(json.dumps({'text': case['text'], 'path': case['path'],
+                    'center': body.get('center', {}).get('id'), 'refused': body.get('refused'),
+                    'also': body.get('also'), 'evidence_count': len(body.get('evidence', [])),
+                    'first_edge': (body.get('evidence') or [{}])[0].get('edge_id')},
+                    ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)
+        slow = ' '.join(['x'] * 250)
+        sequential = []
+        for path, text in [('/api/explore', 'EGFR'), ('/api/explore', slow),
+                           ('/api/ask', slow), ('/api/explore', ' '.join(['x']*244+['osimertinib'])),
+                           ('/api/explore', ' '.join(['x']*251))]:
+            sequential.append(post(path, text))
+        # Four simultaneous allowed 499-character requests contend for the actual engine lock.
+        barrier = threading.Barrier(4)
+        def send_slow(_):
+            barrier.wait()
+            return post('/api/explore', slow)
+        started = time.perf_counter()
+        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
+            concurrent_rows = list(pool.map(send_slow, range(4)))
+        elapsed = time.perf_counter()-started
+        # Readable normal request behind one long lookup; .1 s is a measured scheduling gap,
+        # not a claim that a specific internal lock acquisition has been observed.
+        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
+            future = pool.submit(post, '/api/explore', slow)
+            time.sleep(.1)
+            follower = post('/api/explore', 'EGFR')
+            leader = future.result()
+        result = {'sequential': sequential, 'concurrent': concurrent_rows,
+                  'concurrent_wall_seconds': elapsed, 'normal_behind_slow': {'leader': leader, 'follower': follower},
+                  'normal_after': post('/api/explore', 'EGFR')}
+        (OUT / 'perf_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+        print(json.dumps({'performance_seconds': [r['seconds'] for r in sequential],
+                          'concurrent_seconds': [r['seconds'] for r in concurrent_rows],
+                          'concurrent_wall_seconds': elapsed, 'normal_behind_slow_seconds': follower['seconds']}))
+    finally:
+        proc.terminate()
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+        with socket.socket() as sock:
+            closed = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', PORT)) != 0
+        after = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+        (OUT / 'm5_http_lifecycle.json').write_text(json.dumps({'pid': proc.pid, 'port': PORT,
+            'server_exit': proc.returncode, 'port_closed': closed, 'database_unchanged': before == after,
+            'db_sha256_before': before, 'db_sha256_after': after}, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+        assert closed and before == after

+"""Bounded probe of the answer field passed back into the new linker."""
+import concurrent.futures
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import socket
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:8880'
+DB = ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite'
+
+def post(path, payload, timeout):
+    started = time.perf_counter()
+    try:
+        req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=json.dumps(payload).encode(),
+                                     headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
+        try:
+            response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout)
+        except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
+            response = error
+        return {'status': response.status, 'body': json.loads(response.read()),
+                'seconds': time.perf_counter()-started}
+    except TimeoutError:
+        return {'timeout_seconds': timeout, 'seconds': time.perf_counter()-started}
+
+with socket.socket() as sock:
+    assert sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', 8880)) != 0
+before = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1'}
+with (OUT / 'perf_check_server.txt').open('w', encoding='utf8') as log:
+    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-B', '-u', 'src/app.py', '8880'], cwd=ROOT,
+        env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0))
+    try:
+        for _ in range(100):
+            assert proc.poll() is None
+            try:
+                urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
+                break
+            except OSError:
+                time.sleep(.1)
+        else:
+            raise RuntimeError('server unavailable')
+        normal = post('/api/explore', {'text':'EGFR'}, 5)
+        small = post('/api/check', {'text':'EGFR', 'answer':' '.join(['x']*100)}, 5)
+        payload = {'text':'EGFR', 'answer':' '.join(['x']*500)}
+        slow = post('/api/check', payload, 5)
+        started = time.perf_counter()
+        get = urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=2)
+        get.read()
+        live_get = {'status': get.status, 'seconds': time.perf_counter()-started}
+        follower = post('/api/explore', {'text':'EGFR'}, 2)
+        output = {'normal_before': normal, 'small_check_199_characters': small,
+                  'slow_check_input': payload, 'slow_check_999_characters': slow,
+                  'get_while_check_running': live_get, 'normal_after_check_timeout': follower,
+                  'limitation':'Client timeouts bound the observation; not a completed latency or permanent deadlock claim.'}
+        (OUT / 'perf_check_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
+        print(json.dumps({k: ({x:y for x,y in v.items() if x!='body'} if isinstance(v,dict) else v)
+                          for k,v in output.items() if k!='slow_check_input'}))
+    finally:
+        proc.terminate()
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+        with socket.socket() as sock:
+            closed = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', 8880)) != 0
+        after = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+        (OUT / 'perf_check_lifecycle.json').write_text(json.dumps({'pid':proc.pid, 'port':8880,
+            'port_closed':closed,'database_unchanged':before==after,'server_exit':proc.returncode},indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+        assert closed and before==after

+"""Bounded subprocess benchmarks; writes confined to m5 and never calls a model."""
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+import types
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
+
+if len(sys.argv) > 1:
+    import query
+    import textrules as T
+    mode, kind, size = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3])
+    module = query
+    if mode == 'm4':
+        module = types.ModuleType('perf_m4_query')
+        module.__file__ = str(ROOT / 'src/query.py')
+        sys.modules[module.__name__] = module
+        code = subprocess.run(['git', 'show', 'bf436c6:src/query.py'], cwd=ROOT,
+                              capture_output=True, check=True).stdout.decode('utf-8')
+        exec(compile(code, module.__file__, 'exec'), module.__dict__)
+    kg = module.OncoKG()
+    kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
+    text = {'plain': ' '.join(['x'] * size),
+            'drug': ' '.join(['x'] * size + ['osimertinib']),
+            'split': ';'.join(['x'] * size),
+            'single': 'x' * size,
+            'late_qualifier': ' '.join(['x'] * size + ['biosimilar', 'bevacizumab'])}[kind]
+    calls = {'is_distinct_product': 0, 'is_radiotracer': 0, 'characters': 0}
+    for name in ['is_distinct_product', 'is_radiotracer']:
+        original = getattr(T, name)
+        def wrapped(value, name=name, original=original):
+            calls[name] += 1
+            calls['characters'] += len(value)
+            return original(value)
+        setattr(T, name, wrapped)
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    entities = kg.link_entities(text)
+    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
+    kg.conn.close()
+    print(json.dumps({'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'characters': len(text),
+                     'seconds': elapsed, 'calls': calls, 'entities': entities}))
+else:
+    env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1',
+           'PYTHONIOENCODING': 'utf-8', 'TEMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'),
+           'TMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'), 'TMPDIR': str(OUT / 'tmp')}
+    cases = [('plain', n) for n in [50, 100, 200, 250, 500, 1000]]
+    cases += [('drug', 244), ('split', 250), ('single', 500), ('late_qualifier', 235)]
+    rows = []
+    for kind, size in cases:
+        for mode in ['m4', 'HEAD']:
+            try:
+                proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-B', __file__, mode, kind, str(size)],
+                                      cwd=ROOT, env=env, capture_output=True, timeout=20)
+                row = json.loads(proc.stdout) if proc.returncode == 0 else {
+                    'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'error': proc.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace')}
+            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
+                row = {'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'timeout_seconds': 20}
+            rows.append(row)
+            (OUT / 'perf_results.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+            print(json.dumps(row), flush=True)

exec
